# Skills · P · part 89

881 entries, plan-triage to planejamento-portfolio-marca. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **plan-triage**: Move issues, and external PRs when the repo opts in, through triage states. Lists what needs attention, categorizes, checks for duplicates and prior rejections, verifies the claim, grills open requests with h2xd:plan-grill, and posts agent-ready briefs, triage notes or closing comments through the tracker operations. Use when the user asks to triage, asks what needs attention or is ready for an agent, or names an issue or PR to evaluate or move to a state. ([h2xd/skills](https://github.com/h2xd/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-triage) · ★1)
- **plan-triage**: Use when the user wants to sweep the unfinished plan backlog, detect which plans are functionally complete against the live codebase, and archive the completed ones. Triggers on phrases like "triage my plans", "sweep the plans", "which plans are done", "archive completed plans", "check plan completeness", "auto-archive finished plans", "plan-triage", "sweep the backlog", "clean up the plan backlog". For each non-archived plan under .opencode/plans/, re-verifies every unchecked step against the live tree, runs the plan's own ([jakestrouse00/opencode-toolkit](https://github.com/jakestrouse00/opencode-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-triage))
- **plan-trip**: Guided trip planning workflow. Asks destination, dates, travelers, class, and flexibility, then runs parallel flight/hotel search with cpp math and booking plan. ([borski/travel-hacking-toolkit](https://github.com/borski/travel-hacking-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugins/travel-hacking-toolkit/skills/plan-trip) · ★1k)
- **plan-trip**: Iteratively plans a France Schengen trip with the user — destinations, dates, duration, group composition, rough budget — and surfaces the consular-credibility checks that will affect the visa application later. Outputs a trip skeleton that downstream skills (/itinerary-builder, /document-checklist, /cost-estimate) can consume. Use when the user says "help me plan a trip to France", "I want to go to Europe but don't know where", "we want a Schengen trip but need to figure out details", or starts with a vague idea instead of fixed dates. (Schengen-master skills) ([torlyai/Schengen-master](https://github.com/torlyai/Schengen-master/tree/HEAD/skills/plan-trip) · ★5)
